##### src\app\[locale]\(site)\blog\[slug]\layout.tsx #####
import { BlogCover } from "@/components/aegx/blog-cover";
import { POSTS } from "@/lib/blog-posts";

export default async function PostLayout({ children, params }: { children: React.ReactNode; params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const post = POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
  return (
    <>
      {post ? (
        <section className="aegx-sec">
          <div className="aegx-col">
            <div className="aegx-banner"><BlogCover seed={post.slug} label={post.category} /></div>
          </div>
        </section>
      ) : null}
      {children}
    </>
  );
}

##### src\app\[locale]\(site)\blog\[slug]\page.tsx #####
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { POSTS } from "@/lib/blog-posts";
import { BlogCover } from "@/components/aegx/blog-cover";
import { Cta, Hero, Sec } from "@/components/aegx/ui";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ locale: string; slug: string }> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { slug } = await params;
  const post = POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
  return post ? { title: post.title, description: post.excerpt } : {};
}

export default async function BlogPost({ params }: Props) {
  const { locale, slug } = await params;
  const post = POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
  if (!post) notFound();
  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: "Blog" });
  const de = locale === "de";
  const fmt = (iso: string) =>
    new Date(iso + "T00:00:00Z").toLocaleDateString(de ? "de-DE" : "en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" });
  const more = POSTS.filter((p) => p.slug !== post.slug).slice(0, 3);

  return (
    <>
      <Hero
        compact
        badge={post.category + " \u00b7 " + fmt(post.date) + " \u00b7 " + t("minRead", { n: post.readMinutes })}
        title={post.title}
        intro={post.excerpt}
      />
      <Sec flush>
        <article className="aegx-art">
          {locale !== "en" ? <p className="aegx-art__note">{t("enOnly")}</p> : null}
          {post.body.map((b, i) => {
            if (b.t === "h2") return <h2 key={i} className="aegx-art__h2">{b.text}</h2>;
            if (b.t === "p") return <p key={i} className="aegx-art__p">{b.text}</p>;
            if (b.t === "ul")
              return (
                <ul key={i} className="aegx-art__ul">
                  {b.items.map((it) => <li key={it}>{it}</li>)}
                </ul>
              );
            return <pre key={i} className="aegx-art__code">{b.text}</pre>;
          })}
        </article>
      </Sec>
      {more.length > 0 ? (
        <Sec flush>
          <div className="aegx-tabs"><span style={{ fontSize: 13, color: "#a3a3a3" }}>{t("more")}</span></div>
          <div className="aegx-cells aegx-cells--3">
            {more.map((p) => (
              <Link key={p.slug} href={"/blog/" + p.slug} className="aegx-cell aegx-post">
                <div className="aegx-post__cover"><BlogCover seed={p.slug} label={p.category} /></div>
                <div className="aegx-post__b">
                  <span className="aegx-post__cat">{p.category}</span>
                  <h3 className="aegx-h3">{p.title}</h3>
                  <span className="aegx-post__meta">{fmt(p.date) + " \u00b7 " + t("minRead", { n: p.readMinutes })}</span>
                </div>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Sec>
      ) : null}
      <Cta
        title={t("postCtaTitle")}
        body={t("postCtaBody")}
        primary={{ label: t("test"), href: "/signup" }}
        secondary={{ label: t("enterprise"), href: "/enterprise" }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

##### src\app\[locale]\(site)\blog\page.tsx #####
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { getTranslations } from "next-intl/server";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { POSTS } from "@/lib/blog-posts";
import { BlogCover } from "@/components/aegx/blog-cover";
import { BlogGrid } from "@/components/aegx/blog-grid";
import { Cta, Hero, Sec } from "@/components/aegx/ui";

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ locale: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { locale } = await params;
  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: "Blog" });
  return { title: t("metaTitle"), description: t("metaDesc") };
}

export default async function BlogIndex({ params }: { params: Promise<{ locale: string }> }) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: "Blog" });
  const de = locale === "de";
  const fmt = (iso: string) =>
    new Date(iso + "T00:00:00Z").toLocaleDateString(de ? "de-DE" : "en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" });
  const [first, ...rest] = POSTS;
  const posts = rest.map((p) => ({
    slug: p.slug, title: p.title, excerpt: p.excerpt, category: p.category, date: fmt(p.date), read: t("minRead", { n: p.readMinutes }),
  }));
  return (
    <>
      <Hero compact badge={t("metaTitle")} title={t("title")} intro={t("intro")} />
      {first ? (
        <Sec flush>
          <Link href={"/blog/" + first.slug} className="aegx-feature">
            <div className="aegx-feature__cover"><BlogCover seed={first.slug} label={first.category} /></div>
            <div className="aegx-feature__b">
              <span className="aegx-post__cat">{first.category}</span>
              <h2 className="aegx-h2">{first.title}</h2>
              <p className="aegx-p">{first.excerpt}</p>
              <span className="aegx-post__meta">{fmt(first.date) + " \u00b7 " + t("minRead", { n: first.readMinutes })}</span>
            </div>
          </Link>
          {posts.length > 0 ? <BlogGrid posts={posts} allLabel={de ? "Alle" : "All"} /> : null}
        </Sec>
      ) : null}
      <Cta
        title={t("ctaTitle")}
        body={t("ctaBody")}
        primary={{ label: t("test"), href: "/signup" }}
        secondary={{ label: t("quick"), href: "/docs/quickstart" }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

##### src\app\[locale]\(site)\pricing\page.tsx #####
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Check, Minus } from "lucide-react";
import { getTranslations, setRequestLocale } from "next-intl/server";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { meta } from "@/lib/seo";
import { Btn, Cell, Cells, Cta, Hero, Sec, SectionHead } from "@/components/aegx/ui";

type Props = { params: Promise<{ locale: string }> };

export async function generateMetadata({ params }: Props): Promise<Metadata> {
  const { locale } = await params;
  const t = await getTranslations({ locale, namespace: "Pricing" });
  return meta(t("metaTitle"), t("metaDesc"));
}

type Plan = {
  name: string;
  badge: string | null;
  price: string;
  sub: string;
  blurb: string;
  meta: [string, string][];
  cta: { label: string; href: string };
  more: { label: string; href: string };
  featured: boolean;
};

const GROUP_DEF: { key: string; rows: [string, string, string, string][] }[] = [
  { key: "g1", rows: [["r1", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r2", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r3", "yes", "yes", "planned"]] },
  { key: "g2", rows: [["r4", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r5", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r6", "no", "planned", "planned"]] },
  { key: "g3", rows: [["r7", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r8", "yes", "yes", "planned"], ["r9", "notClaimed", "notClaimed", "planned"]] },
  { key: "g4", rows: [["r10", "no", "no", "no"], ["r11", "no", "no", "no"], ["r12", "no", "yes", "planned"], ["r13", "no", "yes", "planned"]] },
];

type CellLabels = { included: string; notOffered: string; planned: string; notClaimed: string };

function CellView({ v, L }: { v: string; L: CellLabels }) {
  if (v === "yes") return <Check className="aegx-yes" aria-label={L.included} />;
  if (v === "no") return <Minus className="aegx-no" aria-label={L.notOffered} />;
  return <span className="aegx-soft">{v === "planned" ? L.planned : v === "notClaimed" ? L.notClaimed : v}</span>;
}

export default async function PricingPage({ params }: Props) {
  const { locale } = await params;
  setRequestLocale(locale);
  const t = await getTranslations("Pricing");
  const de = locale === "de";

  const L: CellLabels = {
    included: t("cell.included"),
    notOffered: t("cell.notOffered"),
    planned: t("cell.planned"),
    notClaimed: t("cell.notClaimed"),
  };

  const mk = (k: string, badge: string | null, ctaHref: string, moreHref: string, featured: boolean): Plan => ({
    name: t(`plans.${k}.name`),
    badge,
    price: t(`plans.${k}.price`),
    sub: t(`plans.${k}.sub`),
    blurb: t(`plans.${k}.blurb`),
    meta: [
      [t(`plans.${k}.k1`), t(`plans.${k}.v1`)],
      [t(`plans.${k}.k2`), t(`plans.${k}.v2`)],
      [t(`plans.${k}.k3`), t(`plans.${k}.v3`)],
    ],
    cta: { label: t(`plans.${k}.cta`), href: ctaHref },
    more: { label: t(`plans.${k}.more`), href: moreHref },
    featured,
  });

  const PLANS: Plan[] = [
    mk("test", null, "/signup", "/docs/quickstart", false),
    mk("pilot", t("badgeRecommended"), "/enterprise", "/docs", true),
    mk("prod", t("badgePlanned"), "/pilot", "/trust", false),
  ];

  const SCOPE = ["s1", "s2", "s3", "s4", "s5", "s6", "s7", "s8"].map((k) => t(k));
  const FAQ: [string, string][] = ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5"].map((n) => [t("q" + n), t("a" + n)]);

  return (
    <>
      <Hero
        compact
        badge={t("heroEyebrow")}
        title={t("heroTitle")}
        intro={t("heroIntro")}
        primary={{ label: t("primary"), href: "/signup" }}
        secondary={{ label: t("secondary"), href: "/enterprise" }}
      />

      <Sec flush>
        <div className="aegx-pad aegx-pad--head">
          <SectionHead eyebrow={t("plansEyebrow")} title={t("plansTitle")} />
        </div>
        <Cells n={3}>
          {PLANS.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.name} className={"aegx-plan" + (p.featured ? " aegx-plan--feat" : "")}>
              <div className="aegx-plan__top">
                <h3 className="aegx-h3">{p.name}</h3>
                {p.badge ? <span className="aegx-tag">{p.badge}</span> : null}
              </div>
              <p className="aegx-plan__sub">{p.blurb}</p>
              <p className="aegx-price">{p.price}</p>
              <p className="aegx-plan__per">{p.sub}</p>
              <dl className="aegx-meta">
                {p.meta.map(([k, v]) => (
                  <div key={k}>
                    <dt>{k}</dt>
                    <dd>{v}</dd>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </dl>
              <Btn href={p.cta.href} variant={p.featured ? "solid" : "soft"} block>{p.cta.label}</Btn>
              <Link href={p.more.href} className="aegx-more">{p.more.label}</Link>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Cells>

        <div className="aegx-band">
          <div>
            <h3 className="aegx-h3">{"Pro \u00b7 $49" + (de ? " / Monat" : " / month")}</h3>
            <p className="aegx-p">
              {de
                ? "Bis zu 5 Agenten, 5 Mitglieder und 25.000 Entscheidungen pro Monat. Kostenlos starten und jederzeit upgraden."
                : "Up to 5 agents, 5 members and 25,000 decisions per month. Start free and upgrade any time."}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="aegx-band__act">
            <Btn href="/dashboard/billing">{de ? "Pro starten" : "Get Pro"}</Btn>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="aegx-band">
          <div>
            <h3 className="aegx-h3">{t("commTitle")}</h3>
            <p className="aegx-p">{t("commP1")}</p>
            <p className="aegx-p">{t("commP2")}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="aegx-band__act">
            <Btn href="/enterprise">{t("commBtn1")}</Btn>
            <Btn href="/docs" variant="soft">{t("commBtn2")}</Btn>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Sec>

      <Sec flush>
        <div className="aegx-pad aegx-pad--head">
          <SectionHead title={t("scopeTitle")} />
        </div>
        <Cells n={4}>
          {SCOPE.map((s, i) => (
            <Cell key={i} num={String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")} title={s}>{t("agreed")}</Cell>
          ))}
        </Cells>
      </Sec>

      <Sec>
        <SectionHead title={t("compareTitle")} intro={t("compareIntro")} />
        <div className="aegx-tablewrap">
          <table className="aegx-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th />
                {PLANS.map((p) => (<th key={p.name}>{p.name}</th>))}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            {GROUP_DEF.map((g) => (
              <tbody key={g.key}>
                <tr className="aegx-table__group"><th colSpan={4}>{t("groups." + g.key)}</th></tr>
                {g.rows.map((r) => (
                  <tr key={r[0]}>
                    <th scope="row">{t("rows." + r[0])}</th>
                    <td><CellView v={r[1]} L={L} /></td>
                    <td><CellView v={r[2]} L={L} /></td>
                    <td><CellView v={r[3]} L={L} /></td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            ))}
          </table>
        </div>
      </Sec>

      <Sec>
        <SectionHead title={t("faqTitle")} />
        <div className="aegx-faq" style={{ marginTop: 40 }}>
          {FAQ.map(([q, a]) => (
            <details key={q}>
              <summary>{q}<span aria-hidden="true">+</span></summary>
              <p>{a}</p>
            </details>
          ))}
          <p className="aegx-faq__foot">
            {t("faqLead")} <Link href="/pilot">{t("faqLink")}</Link> {t("faqTail")}
          </p>
        </div>
      </Sec>

      <Cta
        title={de ? "Starte mit einem kostenlosen Workspace." : "Start with a free workspace."}
        body={de ? "Verbinde einen Agenten und sende deine erste Testaktion. Upgrade, wenn du bereit bist." : "Connect an agent and send your first test action. Upgrade when you are ready."}
        primary={{ label: de ? "Kostenlos starten" : "Start for free", href: "/signup" }}
        secondary={{ label: de ? "Mit uns sprechen" : "Talk to us", href: "/enterprise" }}
      />
    </>
  );
}

##### src\app\sitemap.ts #####
import type { MetadataRoute } from "next";

const BASE = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL ?? "https://aegisora.org";

const PATHS = [
  "",
  "/about",
  "/blog",
  "/changelog",
  "/contact",
  "/demo",
  "/docs",
  "/docs/quickstart",
  "/docs/technical",
  "/enterprise",
  "/impressum",
  "/pilot",
  "/pricing",
  "/product",
  "/proof",
  "/security",
  "/solutions",
  "/terms",
  "/trust",
  "/trust/privacy",
  "/trust/subprocessors",
];

export default function sitemap(): MetadataRoute.Sitemap {
  return PATHS.map((p) => ({
    url: BASE + p,
    alternates: { languages: { en: BASE + p, de: BASE + "/de" + p } },
    changeFrequency: "monthly" as const,
    priority: p === "" ? 1 : 0.7,
  }));
}

##### src\components\aegisora\bw\bw-footer.tsx #####
import "./aeg-chrome.css";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { SocialLinks } from "@/components/aegisora/social-links";
import { bwFontVars } from "./bw-font-vars";
import { BwBrand } from "./bw-brand";

const COLS: { key: string; links: [string, string][] }[] = [
  {
    key: "product",
    links: [
      ["platform", "/product"],
      ["solutions", "/solutions"],
      ["demo", "/demo"],
      ["pricing", "/pricing"],
      ["enterprise", "/enterprise"],
    ],
  },
  {
    key: "developers",
    links: [
      ["intro", "/docs"],
      ["quickstart", "/docs/quickstart"],
      ["technical", "/docs/technical"],
      ["changelog", "/docs/technical#releases"],
      ["support", "/support"],
    ],
  },
  {
    key: "company",
    links: [
      ["about", "/about"],
      ["contact", "/pilot"],
      ["security", "/security"],
      ["trust", "/trust"],
      ["blog", "/blog"],
    ],
  },
  {
    key: "legal",
    links: [
      ["privacy", "/trust/privacy"],
      ["terms", "/terms"],
      ["impressum", "/impressum"],
      ["subprocessors", "/trust/subprocessors"],
    ],
  },
];

export function BwFooter() {
  const t = useTranslations("Footer");
  return (
    <footer className={"bw-root aegf " + bwFontVars}>
      <div className="aegf__grid">
        <div className="aegf__brand">
          <BwBrand />
          <p className="aegf__tag">{t("tagline")}</p>
          <Link href="/signup" className="aegf__btn">
            {t("getStarted")}
            <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" />
          </Link>
          <p className="aegf__note">{t("note")}</p>
        </div>
        {COLS.map((col) => (
          <nav key={col.key} className="aegf__col" aria-label={t("cols." + col.key)}>
            <h2>{t("cols." + col.key)}</h2>
            <ul>
              {col.links.map(([k, href]) => (
                <li key={k}>
                  <Link href={href}>{t("links." + k)}</Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </nav>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="aegf__social" role="group" aria-label={t("socialAria")}>
        <SocialLinks className="justify-center" />
      </div>
      <div className="aegf__legal">
        <div className="aegf__legalin">
          <span>&copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} Aegisora. {t("rights")}</span>
          <span>{t("sample")} &middot; <a href="https://shaders.paper.design" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Shaders: Paper</a></span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="aegf__word" aria-hidden="true">
        <span>Aegisora</span>
      </div>
    </footer>
  );
}

"use client";

import "./aeg-chrome.css";
import "./bw-drawer.css";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
import { ChevronDown, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link, usePathname } from "@/i18n/navigation";

import { BwBrand } from "./bw-brand";
import { BwLocaleSwitcher } from "./bw-locale-switcher";
import { BwThemeToggle } from "./bw-theme-toggle";

type Child = { key: string; href: string };
type Item = { key: string; href: string; children?: Child[] };

const NAV: Item[] = [
  { key: "home", href: "/" },
  {
    key: "product",
    href: "/product",
    children: [
      { key: "platform", href: "/product" },
      { key: "solutions", href: "/solutions" },
      { key: "proof", href: "/proof" },
      { key: "security", href: "/security" },
      { key: "enterprise", href: "/enterprise" },
      { key: "pilot", href: "/pilot" },
    ],
  },
  { key: "demo", href: "/demo" },
  { key: "pricing", href: "/pricing" },
  {
    key: "docs",
    href: "/docs",
    children: [
      { key: "intro", href: "/docs" },
      { key: "quickstart", href: "/docs/quickstart" },
      { key: "technical", href: "/docs/technical" },
      { key: "developers", href: "/developers" },
      { key: "support", href: "/support" },
      { key: "changelog", href: "/changelog" },
      { key: "blog", href: "/blog" },
    ],
  },
];

export function BwHeader() {
  const t = useTranslations("Nav");
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [drawer, setDrawer] = useState(false);
  const [acc, setAcc] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const burgerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onDoc = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (rootRef.current && !rootRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(null);
    };
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setOpen(null);
        setDrawer(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDoc);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.style.overflow = drawer ? "hidden" : "";
    return () => {
      document.documentElement.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [drawer]);

  /* Pencere genisleyip burger gizlenirse acik menuyu kapat. */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!drawer) return;
    const check = () => {
      const b = burgerRef.current;
      if (b && window.getComputedStyle(b).display === "none") setDrawer(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("resize", check);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", check);
  }, [drawer]);

  const close = () => {
    setOpen(null);
    setDrawer(false);
  };

  const match = (href: string) => (href === "/" ? pathname === "/" : pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(href + "/"));
  const isActive = (item: Item) => (item.href === "/" ? pathname === "/" : [item.href, ...(item.children ?? []).map((c) => c.href)].some(match));

  /* Menu document.body'ye cizilir: header'daki backdrop-filter fixed konumu bozamaz. */
  const drawerEl =
    drawer && typeof document !== "undefined"
      ? createPortal(
          <div className={"bw-root aegd " + bwFontVars} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={t("drawerAria")}>
            <div className="aegd__top">
              <Link href="/" className="aegd__brand" aria-label={t("homeAria")} onClick={close}>
                <BwBrand />
              </Link>
              <BwLocaleSwitcher />
              <BwThemeToggle />
              <button type="button" className="aegd__icon" aria-label={t("menuClose")} onClick={close}>
                <X aria-hidden="true" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="aegd__body">
              {NAV.map((item) => {
                const expanded = !!acc[item.key];
                return (
                  <div key={item.key} className="aegd__group">
                    <div className="aegd__row">
                      <Link href={item.href} className={"aegd__link" + (isActive(item) ? " is-active" : "")} onClick={close}>
                        {t("items." + item.key)}
                      </Link>
                      {item.children ? (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={"aegd__chev" + (expanded ? " is-open" : "")}
                          aria-expanded={expanded}
                          aria-label={t("items." + item.key)}
                          onClick={() => setAcc({ ...acc, [item.key]: !expanded })}
                        >
                          <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" />
                        </button>
                      ) : null}
                    </div>
                    {item.children && expanded ? (
                      <div className="aegd__sub">
                        {item.children.map((c) => (
                          <Link key={c.key} href={c.href} className="aegd__sublink" onClick={close}>
                            {t("items." + c.key)}
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ) : null}
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              <div className="aegd__cta">
                <Link href="/signup" className="aegd__btn" onClick={close}>
                  {t("testAegisora")}
                </Link>
                <Link href="/enterprise" className="aegd__btn aegd__btn--ghost" onClick={close}>
                  {t("contact")}
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )
      : null;

  return (
    <>
      <header className={"bw-root aegh " + bwFontVars}>
        <div className="aegh__bar" ref={rootRef}>
          <Link href="/" className="aegh__brand" aria-label={t("homeAria")} onClick={close}>
            <BwBrand />
          </Link>

          <nav className="aegh__nav" aria-label={t("mainAria")}>
            {NAV.map((item) =>
              item.children ? (
                <div
                  key={item.key}
                  className="aegh__item"
                  onMouseEnter={() => setOpen(item.key)}
                  onMouseLeave={() => setOpen(null)}
                >
                  <Link href={item.href} className={"aegh__link" + (isActive(item) ? " is-active" : "")} onClick={close}>
                    {t("items." + item.key)}
                  </Link>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className={"aegh__chev" + (open === item.key ? " is-open" : "")}
                    aria-label={t("items." + item.key)}
                    aria-haspopup="true"
                    aria-expanded={open === item.key}
                    onClick={() => setOpen(open === item.key ? null : item.key)}
                  >
                    <ChevronDown aria-hidden="true" />
                  </button>
                  {open === item.key ? (
                    <div className="aegh__menu" role="menu">
                      <div className="aegh__menu-in">
                        {item.children.map((c) => (
                          <Link key={c.key} href={c.href} role="menuitem" className="aegh__mi" onClick={close}>
                            <b>{t("items." + c.key)}</b>
                            <span>{t("desc." + c.key)}</span>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div key={item.key} className="aegh__item">
                  <Link href={item.href} className={"aegh__link" + (isActive(item) ? " is-active" : "")} onClick={close}>
                    {t("items." + item.key)}
                  </Link>
                </div>
              ),
            )}
          </nav>

          <div className="aegh__right">
            <BwLocaleSwitcher />
            <BwThemeToggle />
            <Link href="/enterprise" className="aegh__btn aegh__btn--ghost aegh__hide-md" onClick={close}>

##### src\components\aegisora\docs-nav.tsx #####
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl';
import { Link, usePathname } from '@/i18n/navigation';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export type NavItem = { key?: string; title: string; href: string };
export type NavGroup = { key?: string; title: string; items: NavItem[] };

export const NAV_TREE: NavGroup[] = [
  {
    key: 'start',
    title: 'Getting Started',
    items: [
      { key: 'intro', title: 'Introduction', href: '/docs' },
      { key: 'quickstart', title: 'Quickstart', href: '/docs/quickstart' },
      { key: 'policyExample', title: 'Policy Example', href: '/docs#example' },
      { key: 'technical', title: 'Technical reference', href: '/docs/technical' },
    ],
  },
  {
    key: 'arch',
    title: 'Architecture',
    items: [
      { key: 'pipeline', title: 'Runtime Pipeline', href: '/docs#v3' },
      { key: 'pde', title: 'Policies, decisions and evidence', href: '/docs/technical' },
    ],
  },
  {
    key: 'versions',
    title: 'Version History',
    items: [
      { key: 'v1', title: '1.0 - Foundation', href: '/docs#v1' },
      { key: 'v2', title: '2.0 - Context & Risk', href: '/docs#v2' },
      { key: 'v3', title: '3.0 - Enterprise Runtime', href: '/docs#v3' },
      { key: 'v4', title: '4.0 - Current', href: '/docs#v4' },
    ],
  },
  {
    key: 'ref',
    title: 'Reference',
    items: [
      { key: 'capability', title: 'Capability Matrix', href: '/docs#capability' },
      { key: 'api', title: 'API Contract', href: '/docs#api' },
    ],
  },
];

function NavGroupBlock({ group, defaultOpen }: { group: NavGroup; defaultOpen: boolean }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const t = useTranslations('Docs');
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);

  return (
    <div className="border-b border-border/60 pb-1 last:border-0">
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-between py-2.5 text-left transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
      >
        <span className="font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
          {group.key ? t('groups.' + group.key) : group.title}
        </span>
        <ChevronRight
          size={14}
          aria-hidden
          className={cn(
            'shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform duration-300 ease-out',
            open && 'rotate-90'
          )}
        />
      </button>

      <div
        className={cn(
          'grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-in-out',
          open ? 'grid-rows-[1fr]' : 'grid-rows-[0fr]'
        )}
      >
        <div className="overflow-hidden">
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 pb-3 pl-1">
            {group.items.map((item) => {
              // Only the page itself counts as active; #anchor links never do.
              const active = !item.href.includes('#') && pathname === item.href;
              return (
                <li key={item.title}>
                  <Link
                    href={item.href}
                    aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
                    className={cn(
                      'block rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm transition-colors',
                      active
                        ? 'bg-accent font-medium text-accent-foreground'
                        : 'text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent/60 hover:text-foreground'
                    )}
                  >
                    {item.key ? t('items.' + item.key) : item.title}
                  </Link>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function DocsNav() {
  const t = useTranslations('Docs');
  return (
    <nav aria-label={t('aria')} className="max-h-[calc(100vh-7rem)] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
      {NAV_TREE.map((group, i) => (
        <NavGroupBlock key={group.title} group={group} defaultOpen={i < 2} />
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}

##### src\components\aegisora\mobile-nav.tsx #####
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { TouchEvent as ReactTouchEvent } from "react";
import type { LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import {
  Activity, Bot, Check, ClipboardList, Ellipsis, FileCheck, GitBranch, KeyRound,
  LayoutDashboard, Scale, ScrollText, Settings, SlidersHorizontal, TriangleAlert,
  UserCheck, Users, CreditCard, ShieldCheck, Webhook, X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link, usePathname, useRouter } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type Item = { id: string; href: string; label: string; icon: LucideIcon };
type Density = "compact" | "standard" | "large";
type Prefs = { tabs: string[]; density: Density; home: string };

const ITEMS: Item[] = [
  { id: "overview", href: "/dashboard", label: "Overview", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { id: "activity", href: "/dashboard/activity", label: "Live Activity", icon: Activity },
  { id: "agents", href: "/dashboard/agents", label: "Agents", icon: Bot },
  { id: "policies", href: "/dashboard/policies", label: "Policies", icon: ScrollText },
  { id: "decisions", href: "/dashboard/decisions", label: "Decisions", icon: Scale },
  { id: "approvals", href: "/dashboard/approvals", label: "Approvals", icon: UserCheck },
  { id: "incidents", href: "/dashboard/incidents", label: "Incidents", icon: TriangleAlert },
  { id: "evidence", href: "/dashboard/evidence", label: "Evidence", icon: FileCheck },
  { id: "audit", href: "/dashboard/audit", label: "Audit", icon: ClipboardList },
  { id: "traces", href: "/dashboard/traces", label: "Traces", icon: GitBranch },
  { id: "webhooks", href: "/dashboard/webhooks", label: "Webhooks", icon: Webhook },
  { id: "api-keys", href: "/dashboard/api-keys", label: "API Keys", icon: KeyRound },
  { id: "settings", href: "/dashboard/settings", label: "Settings", icon: Settings },
  { id: "team", href: "/dashboard/team", label: "Team", icon: Users },
  { id: "billing", href: "/dashboard/billing", label: "Billing", icon: CreditCard },
  { id: "identity", href: "/dashboard/identity", label: "Identity", icon: ShieldCheck },
];

const GROUPS = [
  { title: "Overview", ids: ["overview"] },
  { title: "Monitor", ids: ["approvals", "activity", "incidents"] },
  { title: "Govern", ids: ["agents", "policies", "evidence"] },
  { title: "Workspace", ids: ["api-keys", "webhooks", "team", "billing", "settings"] },
  { title: "Advanced", ids: ["decisions", "traces", "audit", "identity"] },
];

const DEFAULTS: Prefs = {
  tabs: ["overview", "activity", "approvals", "incidents"],
  density: "standard",
  home: "overview",
};
const KEY = "aeg.dash.prefs.v1";
const byId = (id: string): Item | undefined => ITEMS.find((i) => i.id === id);
const FOCUS = "focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-primary";

function sanitize(raw: unknown): Prefs {
  const r = (raw ?? {}) as Partial<Prefs>;
  const tabs = Array.isArray(r.tabs) ? r.tabs.filter((t) => typeof t === "string" && byId(t)) : [];
  return {
    tabs: tabs.length === 4 ? tabs : DEFAULTS.tabs,
    density: r.density === "compact" || r.density === "large" ? r.density : "standard",
    home: typeof r.home === "string" && byId(r.home) ? r.home : "overview",
  };
}

function tap() {
  try { navigator.vibrate?.(8); } catch { /* not supported */ }
}

export function MobileNav() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState<Prefs>(DEFAULTS);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<null | "more" | "customize">(null);
  const [counts, setCounts] = useState({ approvals: 0, incidents: 0 });
  const sheetRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const closeRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const drag = useRef<{ y: number; dy: number } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let p = DEFAULTS;
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
      if (raw) p = sanitize(JSON.parse(raw));
    } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect -- hydrate prefs from localStorage after mount
    setPrefs(p);
    applyDensity(p.density);
    try {
      const atRoot = window.location.pathname.replace(/^\/[a-z]{2}(?=\/)/, "") === "/dashboard";
      if (atRoot && p.home !== "overview" && !sessionStorage.getItem("aeg.dash.home")) {
        sessionStorage.setItem("aeg.dash.home", "1");
        const it = byId(p.home);
        if (it && window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches) router.replace(it.href);
      }
    } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const save = (next: Prefs) => {
    setPrefs(next);
    applyDensity(next.density);
    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(next)); } catch { /* storage unavailable */ }
  };

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const s = createClient();
      const [a, i] = await Promise.all([
        s.from("approvals").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("status", "pending"),
        s.from("incidents").select("id", { count: "exact", head: true }).eq("status", "open"),
      ]);
      setCounts({ approvals: a.count ?? 0, incidents: i.count ?? 0 });
    } catch { /* badges stay as they are */ }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect -- async fetch, state set after await
    refresh();
    const t = setInterval(refresh, 60000);
    const onVis = () => { if (document.visibilityState === "visible") refresh(); };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    return () => { clearInterval(t); document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis); };
  }, [refresh, pathname]);

  const [sheetPath, setSheetPath] = useState(pathname);
  if (sheetPath !== pathname) { setSheetPath(pathname); setSheet(null); }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sheet) return undefined;
    closeRef.current?.focus();
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") setSheet(null); };
    const prev = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = prev; document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey); };
  }, [sheet]);

  const onTouchStart = (e: ReactTouchEvent) => {
    const t = e.touches[0];
    if (!t) return;
    drag.current = { y: t.clientY, dy: 0 };
  };
  const onTouchMove = (e: ReactTouchEvent) => {
    const d = drag.current;
    const el = sheetRef.current;
    const t = e.touches[0];
    if (!d || !el || !t) return;
    d.dy = Math.max(0, t.clientY - d.y);
    el.style.transition = "none";
    el.style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${d.dy}px, 0)`;
  };
  const onTouchEnd = () => {
    const d = drag.current;
    const el = sheetRef.current;
    drag.current = null;
    if (!d || !el) return;
    el.style.transition = "transform .2s ease-out";
    if (d.dy > 90) setSheet(null);
    else el.style.transform = "";
  };

  const isActive = (it: Item) => (it.id === "overview" ? pathname === it.href : pathname === it.href || pathname.startsWith(it.href + "/"));
  const badge = (id: string) => (id === "approvals" ? counts.approvals : id === "incidents" ? counts.incidents : 0);
  const tabIds = prefs.tabs.length === 4 ? prefs.tabs : DEFAULTS.tabs;
  const tabs = tabIds.map((id) => byId(id)).filter((x): x is Item => !!x);
  const moreActive = !tabs.some(isActive);

  const toggleTab = (id: string) => {
    tap();
    if (prefs.tabs.includes(id)) {
      if (prefs.tabs.length > 1) save({ ...prefs, tabs: prefs.tabs.filter((t) => t !== id) });
      return;
    }
    const next = prefs.tabs.length >= 4 ? [...prefs.tabs.slice(1), id] : [...prefs.tabs, id];
    save({ ...prefs, tabs: next });
  };
  const tabsOk = prefs.tabs.length === 4;

  return (
    <>
      <nav aria-label="Dashboard" className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t border-border bg-background/95 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] backdrop-blur md:hidden">
        <ul className="grid grid-cols-5">
          {tabs.map((it) => {
            const Icon = it.icon;
            const n = badge(it.id);
            const active = isActive(it);
            return (
              <li key={it.id}>
                <Link
                  href={it.href}
                  onClick={tap}
                  aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
                  className={cn(
                    "relative flex min-h-14 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors",
                    FOCUS,
                    active ? "text-primary before:absolute before:inset-x-6 before:top-0 before:h-0.5 before:rounded-full before:bg-primary" : "text-muted-foreground",
                  )}
                >
                  <span className="relative">
                    <Icon className="size-5" />
                    {n > 0 && (
                      <span className="absolute -right-2.5 -top-1.5 min-w-4 rounded-full bg-escalate px-1 text-center text-[10px] font-semibold leading-4 text-black">{n > 99 ? "99+" : n}</span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <span className="max-w-full truncate px-1">{it.label.replace("Live ", "")}</span>
                </Link>
              </li>
            );
          })}
          <li>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { tap(); setSheet("more"); }}
              aria-haspopup="dialog"
              aria-expanded={sheet !== null}
              className={cn("flex min-h-14 w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors", FOCUS, moreActive ? "text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground")}
            >
              <Ellipsis className="size-5" />
              <span>More</span>
            </button>

##### src\components\aegisora\nav-dropdown.tsx #####
'use client';

import { Link } from '@/i18n/navigation';

export type DropdownItem = { title: string; href: string; desc?: string };

export function NavDropdown({ items }: { items: DropdownItem[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="invisible absolute left-0 top-full z-[110] w-[280px] translate-y-1 pt-3 opacity-0 transition-all duration-200 group-hover:visible group-hover:translate-y-0 group-hover:opacity-100">
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#1677ff]/20 bg-[#061022]/98 p-3 shadow-2xl shadow-black/50 backdrop-blur-md">
        <ul className="space-y-0.5">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <li key={item.href}>
              <Link
                href={item.href}
                className="block rounded-lg px-3 py-2 transition-colors hover:bg-[#1677ff]/10"
              >
                <span className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-200">{item.title}</span>
                {item.desc && <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-slate-500">{item.desc}</span>}
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

##### src\components\aegisora\site-header-legacy.tsx #####
"use client";

import { Link, usePathname } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowRight, ChevronDown, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Logo } from "@/components/aegisora/logo";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@/components/aegisora/theme-toggle";
import { SOCIALS } from "@/lib/socials";
import { announcement, headerCtas, mainNav } from "@/lib/main-nav";
import { supportsLight } from "@/lib/theme";

const GITHUB_URL = SOCIALS.find((s) => s.label === "GitHub")?.href ?? "";

const navLink = "text-sm font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground";
const btnPrimary =
  "inline-flex h-9 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition hover:bg-primary-hover";

function isActive(pathname: string, href: string): boolean {
  return pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(href + "/");
}

export function LegacySiteHeader() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const lightOk = supportsLight(pathname);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setMobileOpen(false), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = mobileOpen ? "hidden" : "unset";
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "unset";
    };
  }, [mobileOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setMobileOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <div className="border-b border-border bg-surface-1 text-foreground">
        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-10 max-w-[1280px] items-center justify-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-sm sm:px-6">
          <span className="hidden rounded border border-border bg-background px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground sm:inline">
            {announcement.badge}
          </span>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground">{announcement.text}</span>
          <Link
            href={announcement.href}
            className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 font-medium text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline"
          >
            {announcement.label} <ArrowRight size={14} />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <header className="sticky top-0 z-[100] w-full border-b border-border bg-background/85 text-foreground backdrop-blur">
        <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-[1280px] items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-8">
            <Logo />
            <nav aria-label="Main" className="hidden items-center gap-1 lg:flex">
              {mainNav.map((entry) =>
                entry.items ? (
                  <div key={entry.label} className="group relative">
                    <Link
                      href={entry.href}
                      aria-haspopup="true"
                      aria-current={isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? "page" : undefined}
                      className={
                        "flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 " +
                        navLink +
                        (isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? " text-foreground" : "")
                      }
                    >
                      {entry.label}
                      <ChevronDown
                        size={14}
                        className="transition-transform duration-200 group-focus-within:rotate-180 group-hover:rotate-180"
                      />
                    </Link>
                    <div className="invisible absolute left-0 top-full z-50 w-80 pt-2 opacity-0 transition group-focus-within:visible group-focus-within:opacity-100 group-hover:visible group-hover:opacity-100">
                      <ul className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-popover p-2 shadow-[0_20px_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.25)]">
                        {entry.items.map((item) => (
                          <li key={item.href + item.label}>
                            <Link href={item.href} className="block rounded-lg p-2.5 transition hover:bg-accent">
                              <span className="block text-[15px] font-medium text-foreground">{item.label}</span>
                              {item.desc ? (
                                <span className="mt-0.5 block text-sm leading-snug text-muted-foreground">{item.desc}</span>
                              ) : null}
                            </Link>
                          </li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <Link
                    key={entry.label}
                    href={entry.href}
                    aria-current={isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? "page" : undefined}
                    className={
                      "rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 " + navLink + (isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? " text-foreground" : "")
                    }
                  >
                    {entry.label}
                  </Link>
                ),
              )}
            </nav>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-4 lg:flex">
              {lightOk ? <ThemeToggle /> : null}
              {GITHUB_URL ? (
                <a
                  href={GITHUB_URL}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-md border border-border px-3 text-sm font-medium text-foreground transition hover:border-primary hover:text-primary"
                >
                  GitHub
                </a>
              ) : null}
              <Link href={headerCtas.login.href} className={navLink}>
                {headerCtas.login.label}
              </Link>
              <Link href={headerCtas.primary.href} className={btnPrimary}>
                {headerCtas.primary.label}
              </Link>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-border p-2 text-foreground transition-colors hover:border-primary focus:outline-none lg:hidden"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)}
              aria-label="Toggle menu"
              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
            >
              {mobileOpen ? <X size={22} /> : <Menu size={22} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {mobileOpen ? (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[90] overflow-y-auto bg-background px-6 pb-10 pt-28 text-foreground lg:hidden">
          <nav className="flex flex-col">
            {mainNav.map((entry) => (
              <div key={entry.label} className="border-b border-border py-3">
                <Link href={entry.href} className="block py-1 text-lg font-medium text-foreground">
                  {entry.label}
                </Link>
                {entry.items ? (
                  <ul className="mt-1 space-y-0.5">
                    {entry.items.map((item) => (
                      <li key={item.href + item.label}>
                        <Link href={item.href} className="block rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-base text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent">
                          {item.label}
                        </Link>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            ))}
            <Link href={headerCtas.login.href} className="py-3 text-lg font-medium text-foreground">
              {headerCtas.login.label}
            </Link>
            <Link
              href={headerCtas.primary.href}
              className="inline-flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary text-base font-medium text-primary-foreground"
            >
              {headerCtas.primary.label}
            </Link>
            {lightOk ? (
              <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <span>Theme</span>
                <ThemeToggle />
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </nav>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

##### src\components\aegx\blog-cover.tsx #####
function hash(s: string) {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) { h ^= s.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 16777619); }
  return h >>> 0;
}

/** Deterministic blue glass-slab cover art, generated from the post slug. */
export function BlogCover({ seed, label }: { seed: string; label?: string }) {
  const h = hash(seed);
  const v = h % 4;
  const id = "bc" + h.toString(36);
  const cx = 400 + ((h >> 4) % 70) - 35;
  const w = 168 + v * 12;
  const hh = Math.round(w * 0.5);
  const t = 16;
  const gap = 54 + v * 4;
  const slabs = [0, 1, 2].map((i) => ({ i, x: cx + (v % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * (i - 1) * 16, y: 292 - i * gap }));
  const dots = Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => {
    const r = hash(seed + ":" + i);
    return { x: 30 + (r % 740), y: 20 + ((r >> 8) % 410), o: 0.2 + ((r >> 16) % 50) / 100, r: 1 + ((r >> 20) % 2) };
  });
  return (
    <svg className="aegx-cover" viewBox="0 0 800 450" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" role="img" aria-label={label ?? "Cover"}>
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id={id + "g"} cx="50%" cy="100%" r="75%">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#1f7aff" stopOpacity="0.6" />
          <stop offset="1" stopColor="#1f7aff" stopOpacity="0" />
        </radialGradient>
        <linearGradient id={id + "t"} x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#a8d0ff" stopOpacity="0.62" />
          <stop offset="1" stopColor="#1f7aff" stopOpacity="0.18" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id={id + "l"} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#1f7aff" stopOpacity="0.4" />
          <stop offset="1" stopColor="#0a2a8f" stopOpacity="0.28" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id={id + "r"} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0" stopColor="#0a2a8f" stopOpacity="0.65" />
          <stop offset="1" stopColor="#04123f" stopOpacity="0.4" />
        </linearGradient>
        <pattern id={id + "p"} width="40" height="40" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
          <path d="M40 0H0V40" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" strokeOpacity="0.05" />
        </pattern>
      </defs>
      <rect width="800" height="450" fill="#000" />
      <rect width="800" height="450" fill={"url(#" + id + "p)"} />
      <rect width="800" height="450" fill={"url(#" + id + "g)"} />
      {dots.map((d, i) => (<circle key={i} cx={d.x} cy={d.y} r={d.r} fill="#8fb8ff" opacity={d.o} />))}
      {slabs.map((s) => {
        const top = s.x + "," + (s.y - hh) + " " + (s.x + w) + "," + s.y + " " + s.x + "," + (s.y + hh) + " " + (s.x - w) + "," + s.y;
        const left = (s.x - w) + "," + s.y + " " + s.x + "," + (s.y + hh) + " " + s.x + "," + (s.y + hh + t) + " " + (s.x - w) + "," + (s.y + t);
        const right = (s.x + w) + "," + s.y + " " + s.x + "," + (s.y + hh) + " " + s.x + "," + (s.y + hh + t) + " " + (s.x + w) + "," + (s.y + t);
        return (
          <g key={s.i} className="aegx-cover__s" style={{ animationDelay: s.i * 0.7 + "s" }}>
            <polygon points={left} fill={"url(#" + id + "l)"} stroke="#9cc8ff" strokeOpacity="0.4" />
            <polygon points={right} fill={"url(#" + id + "r)"} stroke="#9cc8ff" strokeOpacity="0.4" />
            <polygon points={top} fill={"url(#" + id + "t)"} stroke="#bcdcff" strokeOpacity="0.7" />
          </g>
        );
      })}
      {label ? (<text x="32" y="46" fill="#8fb4ff" fontSize="13" fontFamily="ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace" letterSpacing="2">{label.toUpperCase()}</text>) : null}
    </svg>
  );
}

##### src\components\aegx\blog-grid.tsx #####
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { BlogCover } from "./blog-cover";

type P = { slug: string; title: string; excerpt: string; category: string; date: string; read: string };

export function BlogGrid({ posts, allLabel }: { posts: P[]; allLabel: string }) {
  const cats = Array.from(new Set(posts.map((p) => p.category)));
  const [cat, setCat] = useState("");
  const list = cat ? posts.filter((p) => p.category === cat) : posts;
  const missing = list.length % 3 === 0 ? 0 : 3 - (list.length % 3);
  return (
    <>
      <div className="aegx-tabs">
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={cat === ""} onClick={() => setCat("")}>{allLabel}</button>
        {cats.map((c) => (<button key={c} type="button" aria-pressed={cat === c} onClick={() => setCat(c)}>{c}</button>))}
      </div>
      <div className="aegx-cells aegx-cells--3">
        {list.map((p) => (
          <Link key={p.slug} href={"/blog/" + p.slug} className="aegx-cell aegx-post">
            <div className="aegx-post__cover"><BlogCover seed={p.slug} label={p.category} /></div>
            <div className="aegx-post__b">
              <span className="aegx-post__cat">{p.category}</span>
              <h3 className="aegx-h3">{p.title}</h3>
              <p className="aegx-p">{p.excerpt}</p>
              <span className="aegx-post__meta">{p.date + " \u00b7 " + p.read}</span>
            </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
        {Array.from({ length: missing }).map((_, i) => (<div key={"f" + i} className="aegx-cell aegx-cell--fill" aria-hidden="true" />))}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

##### src\components\aegx\footer.tsx #####
import { useLocale, useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";

  { key: "legal", links: [["privacy", "/trust/privacy"], ["terms", "/terms"], ["impressum", "/impressum"], ["subprocessors", "/trust/subprocessors"]] },

            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="aegx-foot__legal">
            <span>&copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} Aegisora. {t("rights")}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </>
  );

##### src\components\aegx\header.tsx #####
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useLocale, useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { Link, usePathname } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { BwLocaleSwitcher } from "@/components/aegisora/bw/bw-locale-switcher";
import { AegxBrand } from "./brand";

const NAV = [
  { href: "/product", en: "Product", de: "Produkt" },
  { href: "/solutions", en: "Solutions", de: "L\u00f6sungen" },
  { href: "/pricing", en: "Pricing", de: "Preise" },
  { href: "/docs", en: "Docs", de: "Docs" },
  { href: "/changelog", en: "Updates", de: "Updates" },
  { href: "/blog", en: "Blog", de: "Blog" },
  { href: "/pilot", en: "Contact", de: "Kontakt" },
];

export function AegxHeader() {
  const t = useTranslations("Nav");
  const locale = useLocale();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const de = locale === "de";
  const signIn = de ? "Anmelden" : "Sign in";
  const label = (n: (typeof NAV)[number]) => (de ? n.de : n.en);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => setOpen(false), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return undefined;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") setOpen(false); };
    const prev = document.documentElement.style.overflow;
    document.documentElement.style.overflow = "hidden";
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      document.documentElement.style.overflow = prev;
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    };
  }, [open]);

  const on = (href: string) => pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(href + "/");

  return (
    <>
      <header className="aegx-head">
        <div className="aegx-head__in">
          <Link href="/" className="aegx-head__brand" aria-label={t("homeAria")} onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
            <AegxBrand height={24} />
          </Link>
          <nav className="aegx-nav" aria-label={t("mainAria")}>
            {NAV.map((n) => (
              <Link key={n.href} href={n.href} aria-current={on(n.href) ? "page" : undefined}>{label(n)}</Link>
            ))}
          </nav>
          <div className="aegx-head__right">
            <BwLocaleSwitcher />
            <Link href="/login" className="aegx-head__signin">{signIn}</Link>
            <Link href="/signup" className="aegx-btn aegx-btn--solid aegx-btn--sm">{t("testAegisora")}</Link>
            <button type="button" className="aegx-burger" aria-label={open ? t("menuClose") : t("menuOpen")} aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}>
              {open ? <X aria-hidden="true" /> : <Menu aria-hidden="true" />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>
      {open ? (
        <div className="aegx-drawer" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={t("drawerAria")}>
          <nav aria-label={t("mainAria")}>
            {NAV.map((n) => (
              <Link key={n.href} href={n.href} onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>{label(n)}</Link>
            ))}
          </nav>
          <div className="aegx-drawer__cta">
            <Link href="/login" className="aegx-btn aegx-btn--soft" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>{signIn}</Link>
            <Link href="/signup" className="aegx-btn aegx-btn--solid" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>{t("testAegisora")}</Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

##### src\components\ui\navigation-menu.tsx #####
import { NavigationMenu as NavigationMenuPrimitive } from "@base-ui/react/navigation-menu"
import { cva } from "class-variance-authority"
import { cn } from "cn"
import { ChevronDownIcon } from "lucide-react"

function NavigationMenu({
  align = "start",
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: NavigationMenuPrimitive.Root.Props &
  Pick<NavigationMenuPrimitive.Positioner.Props, "align">) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Root
      data-slot="navigation-menu"
      className={cn(
        "group/navigation-menu relative flex max-w-max flex-1 items-center justify-center",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <NavigationMenuPositioner align={align} />
    </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Root>
  )
}

function NavigationMenuList({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentPropsWithRef<typeof NavigationMenuPrimitive.List>) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.List
      data-slot="navigation-menu-list"
      className={cn(
        "group flex flex-1 list-none items-center justify-center gap-0",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function NavigationMenuItem({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentPropsWithRef<typeof NavigationMenuPrimitive.Item>) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Item
      data-slot="navigation-menu-item"
      className={cn("relative", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

const navigationMenuTriggerStyle = cva(
  "group/navigation-menu-trigger inline-flex h-9 w-max items-center justify-center rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium transition-all outline-none hover:bg-muted focus:bg-muted focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-1 disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 data-popup-open:bg-muted/50 data-popup-open:hover:bg-muted data-open:bg-muted/50 data-open:hover:bg-muted data-open:focus:bg-muted"
)

function NavigationMenuTrigger({
  className,
  children,
  ...props
}: NavigationMenuPrimitive.Trigger.Props) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Trigger
      data-slot="navigation-menu-trigger"
      className={cn(navigationMenuTriggerStyle(), "group", className)}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}{" "}
      <ChevronDownIcon className="relative top-px ml-1 size-3 transition duration-300 group-data-popup-open/navigation-menu-trigger:rotate-180 group-data-open/navigation-menu-trigger:rotate-180" aria-hidden="true" />
    </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Trigger>
  )
}

function NavigationMenuContent({
  className,
  ...props
}: NavigationMenuPrimitive.Content.Props) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Content
      data-slot="navigation-menu-content"
      className={cn(
        "data-ending-style:data-activation-direction=left:translate-x-[50%] data-ending-style:data-activation-direction=right:translate-x-[-50%] data-starting-style:data-activation-direction=left:translate-x-[-50%] data-starting-style:data-activation-direction=right:translate-x-[50%] h-full w-auto p-1 transition-[opacity,transform,translate] duration-[0.35s] ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)] group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:rounded-lg group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:bg-popover group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:text-popover-foreground group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:shadow group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:ring-1 group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:ring-foreground/10 group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:duration-300 data-ending-style:opacity-0 data-starting-style:opacity-0 data-[motion=from-end]:slide-in-from-right-52 data-[motion=from-start]:slide-in-from-left-52 data-[motion=to-end]:slide-out-to-right-52 data-[motion=to-start]:slide-out-to-left-52 data-[motion^=from-]:animate-in data-[motion^=from-]:fade-in data-[motion^=to-]:animate-out data-[motion^=to-]:fade-out **:data-[slot=navigation-menu-link]:focus:ring-0 **:data-[slot=navigation-menu-link]:focus:outline-none group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-open:animate-in group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-open:fade-in-0 group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-open:zoom-in-95 group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-closed:animate-out group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-closed:fade-out-0 group-data-[viewport=false]/navigation-menu:data-closed:zoom-out-95",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function NavigationMenuPositioner({
  className,
  side = "bottom",
  sideOffset = 8,
  align = "start",
  alignOffset = 0,
  ...props
}: NavigationMenuPrimitive.Positioner.Props) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Portal>
      <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Positioner
        side={side}
        sideOffset={sideOffset}
        align={align}
        alignOffset={alignOffset}
        className={cn(
          "isolate z-50 h-(--positioner-height) w-(--positioner-width) max-w-(--available-width) transition-[top,left,right,bottom] duration-[0.35s] ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)] data-instant:transition-none data-[side=bottom]:before:top-[-10px] data-[side=bottom]:before:right-0 data-[side=bottom]:before:left-0",
          className
        )}
        {...props}
      >
        <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Popup className="data-[ending-style]:easing-[ease] xs:w-(--popup-width) relative h-(--popup-height) w-(--popup-width) origin-(--transform-origin) rounded-lg bg-popover text-popover-foreground shadow ring-1 ring-foreground/10 transition-[opacity,transform,width,height,scale,translate] duration-[0.35s] ease-[cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)] outline-none data-ending-style:scale-90 data-ending-style:opacity-0 data-ending-style:duration-150 data-starting-style:scale-90 data-starting-style:opacity-0">
          <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Viewport className="relative size-full overflow-hidden" />
        </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Popup>
      </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Positioner>
    </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Portal>
  )
}

function NavigationMenuLink({
  className,
  ...props
}: NavigationMenuPrimitive.Link.Props) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Link
      data-slot="navigation-menu-link"
      className={cn(
        "flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg p-2 text-sm transition-all outline-none hover:bg-muted focus:bg-muted focus-visible:ring-3 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-1 in-data-[slot=navigation-menu-content]:rounded-md data-active:bg-muted/50 data-active:hover:bg-muted data-active:focus:bg-muted [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function NavigationMenuIndicator({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentPropsWithRef<typeof NavigationMenuPrimitive.Icon>) {
  return (
    <NavigationMenuPrimitive.Icon
      data-slot="navigation-menu-indicator"
      className={cn(
        "top-full z-1 flex h-1.5 items-end justify-center overflow-hidden data-[state=hidden]:animate-out data-[state=hidden]:fade-out data-[state=visible]:animate-in data-[state=visible]:fade-in",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      <div className="relative top-[60%] h-2 w-2 rotate-45 rounded-tl-sm bg-border shadow-md" />
    </NavigationMenuPrimitive.Icon>
  )
}

export {
  NavigationMenu,
  NavigationMenuContent,
  NavigationMenuIndicator,
  NavigationMenuItem,
  NavigationMenuLink,
  NavigationMenuList,
  NavigationMenuTrigger,
  navigationMenuTriggerStyle,
  NavigationMenuPositioner,
}

"use client";

import "@/styles/zp.css";
import { Link } from "@/i18n/navigation";

##### src\components\zp\header.tsx #####
"use client";

import "@/styles/zp.css";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, Menu, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Link, usePathname } from "@/i18n/navigation";
import { Logo } from "@/components/aegisora/logo";
import { ZpMotion } from "@/components/zp/motion";
import { supportsLight } from "@/lib/theme";
import { ThemeToggle } from "@/components/aegisora/theme-toggle";
import { SOCIALS } from "@/lib/socials";
import { headerCtas, mainNav } from "@/lib/main-nav";

const GITHUB_URL = SOCIALS.find((s) => s.label === "GitHub")?.href ?? "";

function isActive(pathname: string, href: string): boolean {
  return pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(href + "/");
}

export function ZpHeader() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const lightOk = supportsLight(pathname);
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setMobileOpen(false), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = mobileOpen ? "hidden" : "";
    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [mobileOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setMobileOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <ZpMotion />


      <header className="zp zp-header">
        <div className="zp-container zp-header-in">
          <div className="zp-header-l">
            <Logo />
            <nav aria-label="Main" className="zp-nav">
              {mainNav.map((entry) =>
                entry.items ? (
                  <div key={entry.label} className="zp-nav-item">
                    <Link
                      href={entry.href}
                      aria-haspopup="true"
                      aria-current={isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? "page" : undefined}
                      className="zp-nav-link"
                    >
                      {entry.label}
                      <ChevronDown size={14} className="zp-chev" aria-hidden />
                    </Link>
                    <div className="zp-mega">
                      <div className="zp-container zp-mega-in">
                        <div>
                          <p className="zp-mega-title">{entry.label}</p>
                          <p className="zp-mega-sub">Everything in one place.</p>
                        </div>
                        <ul className="zp-mega-grid">
                          {entry.items.map((item) => (
                            <li key={item.href + item.label}>
                              <Link href={item.href} className="zp-mega-item">
                                <b>{item.label}</b>
                                {item.desc ? <span>{item.desc}</span> : null}
                              </Link>
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div key={entry.label} className="zp-nav-item">
                    <Link
                      href={entry.href}
                      aria-current={isActive(pathname, entry.href) ? "page" : undefined}
                      className="zp-nav-link"
                    >
                      {entry.label}
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                ),
              )}
            </nav>
          </div>

          <div className="zp-header-r">
            <Link href={headerCtas.primary.href} className="zp-btn zp-btn--primary zp-btn--sm zp-m-cta">
              {headerCtas.primary.label}
            </Link>
            <div className="zp-header-cta">
              {lightOk ? <ThemeToggle /> : null}
              {GITHUB_URL ? (
                <a href={GITHUB_URL} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="zp-gh">
                  GitHub
                </a>
              ) : null}
              <Link href={headerCtas.login.href} className="zp-header-link">
                {headerCtas.login.label}
              </Link>
              <Link href={headerCtas.primary.href} className="zp-btn zp-btn--primary zp-btn--sm">
                {headerCtas.primary.label}
              </Link>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="zp-burger"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)}
              aria-label="Toggle menu"
              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
            >
              {mobileOpen ? <X size={20} /> : <Menu size={20} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {mobileOpen ? (
        <div className="zp zp-mobile">
          <nav aria-label="Mobile">
            {mainNav.map((entry) => (
              <div key={entry.label} className="zp-mobile-group">
                <Link href={entry.href}>{entry.label}</Link>
                {entry.items ? (
                  <ul>
                    {entry.items.map((item) => (
                      <li key={item.href + item.label}>
                        <Link href={item.href}>{item.label}</Link>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            ))}
            <div className="zp-mobile-cta">
              <Link href={headerCtas.primary.href} className="zp-btn zp-btn--primary">
                {headerCtas.primary.label}
              </Link>
              <Link href={headerCtas.login.href} className="zp-btn zp-btn--outline">
                {headerCtas.login.label}
              </Link>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", fontSize: 14 }}>
                <span className="zp-muted">Theme</span>
                {lightOk ? <ThemeToggle /> : null}
              </div>
            </div>
          </nav>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}

##### src\lib\aegisora\nav.ts #####
import { Activity, Bot, LayoutDashboard, ScrollText, Settings, UserCheck, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";

export type NavTab = { label: string; href: string };
export type NavGroup = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  href: string;
  icon: LucideIcon;
  prefixes: string[];
  tabs?: NavTab[];
};

export const MAIN_NAV: NavGroup[] = [
  { id: "overview", label: "Overview", href: "/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard, prefixes: [] },
  { id: "agents", label: "Agents", href: "/dashboard/agents", icon: Bot, prefixes: ["/dashboard/agents"] },
  { id: "approvals", label: "Approvals", href: "/dashboard/approvals", icon: UserCheck, prefixes: ["/dashboard/approvals"] },
  { id: "policies", label: "Policies", href: "/dashboard/policies", icon: ScrollText, prefixes: ["/dashboard/policies"] },
  {
    id: "activity",
    label: "Activity",
    href: "/dashboard/activity",
    icon: Activity,
    prefixes: [
      "/dashboard/activity", "/dashboard/decisions", "/dashboard/incidents",
      "/dashboard/traces", "/dashboard/audit", "/dashboard/evidence",
    ],
    tabs: [
      { label: "Live", href: "/dashboard/activity" },
      { label: "Decisions", href: "/dashboard/decisions" },
      { label: "Incidents", href: "/dashboard/incidents" },
      { label: "Traces", href: "/dashboard/traces" },
      { label: "Audit", href: "/dashboard/audit" },
      { label: "Evidence", href: "/dashboard/evidence" },
    ],
  },
];

export const SETTINGS_NAV: NavGroup = {
  id: "settings",
  label: "Settings",
  href: "/dashboard/settings",
  icon: Settings,
  prefixes: [
    "/dashboard/settings", "/dashboard/workspace", "/dashboard/team", "/dashboard/api-keys",
    "/dashboard/webhooks", "/dashboard/billing", "/dashboard/identity",
  ],
  tabs: [
    { label: "General", href: "/dashboard/settings" },
    { label: "Workspace", href: "/dashboard/workspace" },
    { label: "Team", href: "/dashboard/team" },
    { label: "API keys", href: "/dashboard/api-keys" },
    { label: "Webhooks", href: "/dashboard/webhooks" },
    { label: "Billing", href: "/dashboard/billing" },
    { label: "Identity", href: "/dashboard/identity" },
  ],
};

export const ALL_NAV: NavGroup[] = [...MAIN_NAV, SETTINGS_NAV];

export function activeGroup(pathname: string): NavGroup | null {
  const p = pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/";
  if (p === "/dashboard") return MAIN_NAV[0];
  return ALL_NAV.find((g) => g.id !== "overview" && g.prefixes.some((x) => p === x || p.startsWith(x + "/"))) ?? null;
}

##### src\lib\blog.ts #####
export type Block =
  | { t: "p"; text: string }
  | { t: "h2"; text: string }
  | { t: "ul"; items: string[] }
  | { t: "code"; code: string; caption?: string };

export type Post = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  excerpt: string;
  category: string;
  date: string;
  hue: string;
  body: Block[];
};

      { t: "ul", items: ["effect: ALLOW, BLOCK or ESCALATE", "decision_id: the identifier of this decision", "approval_id: present when the effect is ESCALATE", "evidence_hash: the hash of the evidence entry for this decision"] },
      { t: "h2", text: "What to expect first" },
      { t: "p", text: "Without a matching policy the default is BLOCK. Add a policy for the action and send the same request again to see the decision change." },
    ],
  },
];

export function getPost(slug: string) {
  return POSTS.find((p) => p.slug === slug);
}

export function readingMinutes(p: Post) {
  const words = p.body
    .map((b) => (b.t === "ul" ? b.items.join(" ") : b.t === "code" ? "" : b.text))
    .join(" ")
    .split(/\s+/).length;
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(words / 200));
}

export function slugify(s: string) {
  return s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/(^-|-$)/g, "");
}

##### src\lib\blog-posts.ts #####
export type Block =
  | { t: "h2"; text: string }
  | { t: "p"; text: string }
  | { t: "ul"; items: string[] }
  | { t: "code"; text: string };

export type Post = {
  slug: string;
  title: string;
  date: string;
  readMinutes: number;
  category: string;
  excerpt: string;
  body: Block[];
};

export function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso + "T00:00:00Z").toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  });
}
